Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the monte_carlo framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 780. The resulting operator achieves bound O(319/n^2).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 780 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the monte_carlo structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 319. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
